Text is structured in tables and labeled blocks rather than continuous prose.

## Visual Summary (Page 15)

- page_class: timing_diagram
- confidence: 0.92
- reason_codes: timing_diagram_detected, dense_circuit_layout, signal_labels_present

Page 19 contains a detailed STLINK-V3EC hardware schematic with multiple subsystem blocks including USB interface, MCU power management, overvoltage protection circuits, and LED status indicators. The diagram uses standard symbols for capacitors (C), resistors (R), transistors (T), diodes (D), and integrated circuits (U). Key sections include STLINK-V3EC ADC/USB controller block, MCU interface with target MCU via SWD0/SWD1 pins, power regulation stages using TPS25953DSG and TPS25953DSG backup regulators, LED drivers for status indication, and protection logic for overvoltage/overcurrent conditions. The layout is organized into functional blocks connected by signal lines with annotations for pin numbers, component values, and control signals.
